māṇikka viḻumiyaṅkaḷ- 15

mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār

veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ  patippakam
eṇ. 2,  ciṅkāravēlar teru,
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017.
to.pē.: 24339030, cel: 9444410654
miṉṉañcal: naṭayeyaṇāyapayavēa@paayaṭi.uṭia


tāḷ : 16.0 ki. mēplittō,  aḷavu : 1/8 temmi 
 eḻuttu : 11.5 puḷḷi,  pakkam : 368  kaṭṭamippu : iyalpu  paṭikaḷ : 1000  nūlākkam : kō. cittirā, pa. kayalviḻi 
 aṭṭi vaṭivamippu : celvaṉ pā. ari (hariṣ) 
 accu : veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ,  
āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉi - 600 006.

nuḻivuri
tamiḻ marapukaḷit talimuṟi talimuṟiyākak kāttu varum tamiḻccamūkaṅkaḷil taliyāya camūkam nāṭṭuk kōṭṭi nakarattār camūkam. intac camūkam īṉṟeṭutta arumperum aṟiñarum, nāṭu, moḻi, iṉam, camutāyam, kalvi, ilakkiyam, vāḻvu mutalāṉa palvēṟu poruḷkaḷip paṟṟit tam teḷḷat teḷinta cintaṉikaḷāl tamiḻ ilakkiya vāṉil cintaṉic ciṟpiyāka valam vantavar mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār āvār.
ipperuntamiḻ ācāṉ vāḻnta kālattil tamiḻarkku eyppil vippāka eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuccelvaṅkaḷit tokuttu mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa.māṇikkaṉāriṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivip pōṟṟum vakiyil māṇikka viḻumiyaṅkaḷ eṉum talippil 18 tokutikaḷāka tamiḻkūṟum nallulakam payaṉ koḷḷum vakiyil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.
“mūtaṟiñar māṇikkaṉār tamiḻ ārāycci ulakil tamiḻp pēroḷiyāy viḷaṅkiyavar; avaruṭiya āyvu neṟi tamiḻ marapu cārntatu. tolkāppiyattiṉ putumik kūṟukaḷiyum, pāratiyiṉ paḻamik kūṟukaḷiyum kaṇṭu kāṭṭiyavar. uriyāciriyarkaḷip paṟṟi nirampac cintitta uriyāciriyar. tolkāppiyattiṟkum, tirukkuṟaḷukkum teḷivuri kaṇṭavar. marapuvaḻip pulamiyiṉ kaṭici vaḷiyamākat tikaḻntavar, āyvu vaṉmikkut `tamiḻkkātal’, cintaṉit teḷiviṟku `vaḷḷuvam’, pulami nalattiṟkuk `kampar’ ākiya ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷit tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷ iṉam kāṭṭi makiḻvar. ” eṉṟu maṇivācakar patippakattiṉ niṟuvaṉar ayyā ca. meyyappaṉ avarkaḷ ipperuntamiḻ ācāṉiṉ aṟiviṉ parappi viyantu pēcukiṟār.
vaḷarntu varum tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattiṟku va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār patitta patippuc cuvaṭukaḷ putiya vaḻikāṭṭa vallaṉa. tām eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmiyi nilināṭṭiyavar. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiṉ āyvut tiṟaṉum koḷki uṟutiyum; tiru.vi.ka.viṉ moḻinaṭiyum camutāya nōkkum ivar nūlkaḷil mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittokuppukaḷip payilvōr ivvuṇmiyi muḻutāy uṇara muṭiyum.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻ - tamiḻarkkup perumi cērkkum payaṉuḷḷa nalla tamiḻ nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil taṉittaṉmiyuḷḷa niṟuvaṉam eṉpati niṟuvi varukiṟatu. itaṉ perumikkup perumi cērkkum vakiyil mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvataṉ mūlam uvakiyum, kaḷippum, pūrippum aṭikiṟēṉ. ivar nūlkaḷi nāṅkaḷ veḷiyiṭuvati eṅkaḷukkuk kiṭitta naṟpēṟāka eṇṇi eṇṇi makiḻkiṟēṉ.
nalla tamiḻ nūlkaḷip patippittut tamiḻkūṟum ulakiṟku aḷikkum pōtellām eṉ cinti makiḻkiṟatu; puttuṇarvum, pūrippum, putteḻucciyum aṭikiṟēṉ. tamiḻp patippu ulakil uyarvutarum neṟikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷa uṟuti ēṟkiṟēṉ. 
naṉṟikkuriyavarkaḷ
maṇivācakar patippaka niṟuvaṉar ayyā ca. meyyappaṉ avarkaḷ ivvaruntamiḻ aṟiñar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum aruntoṇṭu ceytavar. inta nērattil avari naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ. 
pāri niliyam maṟṟum kārikkuṭi celvi puttaka niliyam ākiya iraṇṭum peruntamiḻ aṟiñar va.cupa.mā. nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻ ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatiyum innērattil naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ. 
pēraṟiñariṉ makkaḷ aṉivarum innūl tokuppukaḷ ellā niliyilum ceppamuṭaṉ veḷivaruvataṟku utaviṉar. iccentamiḻt tokuppukaḷ veḷivaruvataṟku mūla viciyāy amintavarkaḷ tirumati. teṉṟal aḻakappaṉ, tirumati mātari veḷḷiyappaṉ ākiya ivviru peṇmakkaḷ  māṇikka viḻumiyaṅkaḷ tamiḻ ulakam payaṉ koḷḷum vakiyil veḷivaruvataṟkut tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāka iruntati naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu  niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.
moḻiyi mūccākavum, aṟatti vāḻvākavum, ilakkiya vēḷviyi viṉiyākavum ākkik  koṇṭavar mūtaṟiñar va.cupa.mā avarkaḷ eṉpati mēliccivapuri, kaṇēcar kali aṟiviyal kallūri tamiḻ uyarāyvu miyam iṉam kāṭṭi uḷḷatu. 
itu mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār kūṟiya pukaḻ varikaḷ.
“māṇikkac celva maṇittamiḻ aṉṉikkuk
  kāṇikki yāyvāytta kaṇṇāḷa - pēṇikki
  vitta tuṟiyellām vāynta tuṟiyākki
  vittanī eṅkaḷiṉ vippu”
ippāṭal varikaḷukku ēṟpa vāḻntu kāṭṭiya  niṟitamiḻ aṟiñariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkuvataṉ mūlam niṟivaṭikiṟēṉ.
“taṉakkeṉa vāḻvatu cāvukku oppākum
      tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻvatē vāḻvatākum”
eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷi niṉivil koḷvōm.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ


nūlākkattiṟkut tuṇi niṉṟōr
kaṇiṉi ceytōr:
tirumati kō. cittirā	tirumati pa. kayalviḻi
tirumati vi. hēmalatā	tiru. ṭi. irattiraṉarācu
nūl vaṭivamippu:  tirumati  kō. cittirā
mēlaṭṭi vaṭivamippu: celvaṉ pā. ari (hariṣ)
mutal tiruttatiṟku utaviyōr:
tirumati. teṉṟal aḻakappaṉ	tiru. pulavar. ta. āṟumukam tiru. ka. karuppiyā	muṉivar. ka. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ	pulavar. mu. irācavēlu	tiru. nāka. cokkaliṅkam
iṟutit tiruttam :	muṉivar mātari veḷḷiyappaṉ
nūlākkattiṟku utaviyōr:
irā. paramēcuvaraṉ, ku. marutu, vi. matimāṟaṉ
accaṭippu maṟṟum nūl kaṭṭamippu:
veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭarcu, āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉi.
`māṇikka viḻumiyaṅkaḷ’ ellā niliyilum ceppamuṟa veḷivaruvataṟkup pallāṟṟāṉum utaviyāka irunta ivarkaḷukkum maṟṟum aṟiñar perumakkaḷukkum  em neñcam niṟinta naṉṟiyum pārāṭṭum.
 


aṟimukavuri
cemmal va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār
tamiḻccāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ muṉṉōr eṭuttu vitta aṭiccuvaṭṭil tāmum kāl patittu eḻuttukkaḷiyum āyvukaḷiyum ceyta kālattil taṉakkeṉa oru taṉi vaḻiyi amittukkoṇṭavar cemmal avarkaḷ. tamiḻakattil piṟantu iraṅkūṉil vaḷarntu vāymik kuṟikkōḷit taṉ vāḻviṉ kuṟikkōḷāka eṇṇiya paḷḷippaṭippi niṟivu ceyyāta oru ciṟuvaṉi aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭāḷ tamiḻaṉṉi. acciṟuvaṉit tamiḻkkoṭiyāka ulakiṟkut tantāḷ. paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār tuṇi koṇṭu tamiḻik kacaṭaṟak kaṟṟavar, va.cupa. collum ceyalum oṉṟāka vāḻntavar.
tamiḻkkoṭiyāka vanta va.cupa. tamiḻukkuc ceyta koṭi mika atikam. tamiḻāl aṭiyāḷam kāṇappeṟṟa avar kaṟṟōrukku mutaṉmiyarākavum ellōrukkum talivarākavum viḷaṅkiṉār. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷi putiya nōkkil āyvu ceytavar. iṉi varum kālaṅkaḷil caṅka ilakkiyam kaṟpāriṉ nuḻivāyil va.cupa.viṉ akattiṇi āyvu nūl `tamiḻkkātal’.
irāmāyaṇattil paṟṟillāmal iruntavarkaḷiyum `kampar’ eṉṟa nūl kamparāmāyaṇattik kaṟkat tūṇṭukiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattiṉ peyarkkāraṇatti āyvu ceytatu `entac cilampu’. tirukkuṟaḷip putiya kōṇattil āyvu kaṇṭatu `vaḷḷuvam’ maṟṟum `tirukkuṟaṭcuṭar’. tirukkuṟaḷiṉ mūlamum uriyum oṭṭippiṟanta iraṭṭiyar pōl kāṇum `uri naṭiyil tirukkuṟaḷ’. tolkāppiyatti veḷiyulakiṟkuk koṇṭu vantu aṟiñarkaḷiṭiyē niliniṟuttiyavar va.cupa. 
tamiḻkkoṭi maṭṭumaṉṟu oru tamiḻāciriyaṉālum aṟakkoṭi ceyya muṭiyumeṉpati nilināṭṭiyavar. aṟam ceyya paṇam vēṇṭiyatilli. maṉam tāṉ vēṇṭum. aḻippu vēṇṭiyatilli. uḻipputtāṉ vēṇṭum. aṟivu kūṭa vēṇṭiyatilli. aṉpu kūṭa vēṇṭum. eṉpati uṇarttiyavar. cemmal va.cupa. avarkaḷiṉ pukaḻ tamiḻ vāḻum kālamellām nilittu niṟkum.
anniya mōkattil aṭimippaṭṭu, urimi keṭṭu, āṭciatikāram tolittu allaluṟṟukkiṭanta intiya maṇṇil, akkiṉik kuñcukaḷi vaḷartteṭutta aṇṇal kāntikkup pārati pāṭiya vāḻttup pā itu:
“vāḻka nī! emmāṉ, inta viyattu nāṭṭi lellām
  tāḻvuṟṟu vaṟumi miñci viṭutali tavaṟik keṭṭup
  pāḻpaṭṭu niṉṟa tāmōr pārata tēcan taṉṉi
 vāḻvikka vanta kānti makātmā! nī vāḻka vāḻka!”
itip pōlavē, tamiḻ maṇṇil anniya moḻikaḷiṉ ātikkattiṟkut tamiḻar aṭipaṇintu, moḻi urimi iḻantu, tamiḻk kalvikkup peruṅkēṭu nērnta pōtu, inta avalatti māṟṟum muyaṟciyil taṉṉiyē arppaṇittuk koṇṭavar aṇṇal va.cupa.mā.
aṇṇal kāntiyi pārati pāṭiyatupōl, aṇṇal māṇikkaṉāri,
“vāḻka nī emmāṉ, iṅku vantēṟi moḻimō kattāl
  tāḻvuṟṟu urimi keṭṭut tamiḻkkalvi taṉiyum viṭṭup
  pāḻpaṭṭup parita vitta pintamiḻ taṉṉik kāttu
  vāḻvikka vanta putiya vaḷḷuvamē vāḻka! vāḻka!
eṉṟu pāṭip paravat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
- teṉṟal aḻakappaṉ
(va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ makaḷ)



māṇikkam - tamiḻ vārppu
vāymi vāḻvit taṉ uyirākavum, tiruvācakam, tirukkuṟaḷ, tolkāppiyam mūṉṟiṉiyum pōṟṟip pātukāttu poyyāmi, tūymi, nērmi, eḷimi eṉṟa nāṉku tūṇkaḷiyum aṭittaḷamākavum varalāṟākavum koṇṭu vāḻntavar tamiḻc cemmal, mūtaṟiñar mutu pērāyvāḷar, peruntamiḻk kāvalar, mutuperum pulavar, tolkāppiyat takiñar eṉatu tanti va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār.
peṟṟōrkaḷ illāmalē vaḷarntālum taṉṉi naṟpaṇpukaḷālum nalloḻukkattālum naṟcintaṉikaḷālum cetukkik koṇṭavarkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ maṟiviṟkup piṉ tāyār tirumati. ēkammi māṇikkam avarkaḷ tanti iṭṭa puḷḷikaḷik kōlamākkiṉārkaḷ. appā taṉatu vāḻnāḷil 50 āṇṭukaṭku mēlāka tamiḻip pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ. tāyār (tantiyiṉ maṟiviṟkup piṟaku) 20 āṇṭukaṭku mēlāka tantiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷkaḷip pōṟṟi niṟivēṟṟiṉārkaḷ. tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum iṉṟu tantiyip pōṟṟukiṟārkaḷ.
cemmaliṉ aṟivuṭimi tamiḻaka aracāl potu vuṭimi ākkappaṭṭatu. eṉ tantiyiṉ toṇṭukaḷ palatiṟattaṉa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāviṉ nilippayaṉāka cemmaliṉ nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum maṟupaṭiyum patippittu avaṟṟi tamiḻulakiṟkuk kiṭikkumpaṭi ceyya vēṇṭum eṉavum cemmaliṉ paṭippukkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvatu viḻāviṟku uyarvum nūlkaḷ vaḻivaḻi kiṭikkavum tēṭaliṉṟik  kiṭikkavum vaḻi vakukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum makkaḷ nāṅkaḷ eṇṇiṉōm.
nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāp paṇiyāka  māṇikkaṉār nūlkaḷi yellām veḷiyiṭum amiyattu cemmaliṉ tamiḻmiyi tiṟaṉāyntu tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ muttirikaḷum nūlkaḷāka  veḷivarukiṟatu. ivvāṇṭil poṟuppeṭuttu veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṟkum em tantiyiṉ tamiḻppaṇik kuṟittu perumi tarum nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyuḷḷa  nūlāciriyar kaṭkum tamiḻiṉamē naṉṟi kūṟum.
muṉivar. mātari veḷḷiyappaṉ
(va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ makaḷ)
kōvi	
11.8.2017
eṉ aṉpuṟi
eṭṭu vayatu pālakaṉāka iruntapōtē īṉṟeṭutta peṟṟōrkaḷ viṇṇulaku eytiya cūḻnili.
iḷam vayatilēyē “lēvātēvi” eṉṟu collappaṭum vaṭṭit toḻilik kaṟṟuk koḷḷa parmā ceṉṟu vēlippārtta iṭattil mutalāḷi, varumāṉavari atikāriyiṭam poy collumpaṭi appāvi vaṟpuṟuttiyatāl, tiṇṇamāka maṟuttuviṭṭut tāyakam tirumpiya uttama maṉitar.
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattil paṇṭitamaṇi, katirēcaṉāriṭam tamiḻ payiṉṟu, piṟkālattil kārikkuṭiyil tāṉ kaṭṭiya illattiṟku “katirakam” eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭi, ācāṉukku mariyāti celuttiya māmaṉitar.
ciṟpi oru kalli uḷiyik koṇṭu cetukkic cetukki, ciṟanta ciṟpamākkuvāṉ. atēpōl, taṉ maṉati eḷiya vāḻvu, etirkāla nampikki, teḷivāṉa cintaṉikaḷ, vāḻkkikkāṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ, uyarnta kuṟikkōḷ, uṉṉata māṉa ceyal, vāymi, tūymi, nērmi, iṟi vaḻipāṭu pōṉṟa eṇṇat tiṇmiyuṭaṉ taṉṉittāṉē pakkuvap paṭuttik koṇṭatāl, uyar pataviyāṉa tuṇivēntar patavi appāvi nāṭi vantatu.
tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvācakam, tolkāppiyam ākiya mūṉṟu tamiḻ maṟikaḷum, paṇpuṭaṉ vāḻvataṟkuriya vaḻikāṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu niṉittu vāḻvil kaṭippiṭittu veṟṟi kaṇṭa uyarnta maṉitar.
appā eḻutiyuḷḷa `taṟcintaṉi’ eṉṉum kuṟippēṭṭil “nāṉ paṭippilum eḻuttilum āyvilum patavip paṇiyilum muḻu nēramum īṭupaṭumāṟu kuṭumpap poṟuppi muḻutum tāṅkiyavaḷ ēkammi eṉṟa eṉ orē maṉivi” eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷatiliruntu appāviṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku ammā muḻu uṟutuṇiyāka iruntuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpati nāṉ kuṟippiṭa virumpukiṟēṉ.
cumār aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē kalvikku mika mukkiyattuvam koṭukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa eṇṇam eṉ tāytantiyar iruvarukkum orumittak karuttāka iruntatāl, taṉ kuḻantikaḷ aṟuvariyum paḷḷip paṭippōṭu niṟuttiviṭāmal, āṇ, peṇ eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu illāmal paṭṭap paṭippukkaḷ vari payila vittu veṟṟi kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
eṉ iḷaṅkali vaṇikaviyal cāṉṟitaḻilum, eṉ kaṇavariṉ poṟiyiyal cāṉṟitaḻilum tuṇivēntariṉ kiyoppa iṭattil eṉ tantiyāriṉ kiyoppam irukkum. iti nāṉ peṟṟa pākkiyamāka niṉikkiṟēṉ.
nāṉ peṅkaḷūril iruntapōtu, appā eḻutiya ovvoru kaṭitattilum, “avinayaṉ tirukkuṟaḷ collu kiṟāṉā? putitākac collum kuṟaḷukku ayyā kācu taruvārkaḷ eṉṟu col” eṉṟa varikaḷ niccayamāka irukkum. eṉ makaṉ iḷam vayatilēyē tirukkuṟaḷ kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ tantiyāriṉ eṇṇam.
iḷañciṟārkaḷukkup piñcu neñcaṅkaḷilēyē tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvācakam paṭittal, iṟivaḻipāṭu ceytal pōṉṟa vitikaḷi vitittu viṭṭāl, muḻuvāḻvum mikavum ciṟappāka amiyum eṉpatu tantiyāriṉ tiṇmiyāṉa karuttu.
mikac ciṟu vayatil peṟṟōri iḻanta cūḻniliyil, aṭippaṭi poruḷātāram illāta niliyil, atikamāṉa uḻippālum, muyaṟciyālum, nērmiyāṉa vaḻiyil tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷātārattil, atāvatu taṉ cottil āṟil oru paṅki taṉatu conta ūrāṉa mēliccivapuriyiṉ mēmpāṭṭukkāka uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi pākupāṭuyiṉṟi celavu ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu taṉ viruppamuṟiyil eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tantiyāriṉ viruppappaṭiyē tāyār avarkaḷ 20-4-1992 il appāviṉ peyaril aṟakkaṭṭaḷi ārampittu naṭatti vantārkaḷ. iṉṟum toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟu varukiṟatu.
appā eḻutiya muppatukkum mēlāṉa nūlkaḷil `vaḷḷuvam’, `tamiḻkkātal’, `kampar’, `cintaṉikkaḷaṅkaḷ’ pōṉṟavi tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟil mikacciṟanta āyvu nūlkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.
uṉṉata vāḻkkikku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāka vāḻntuḷḷa eṉ tantiyāriṉ vāḻkki varalāṟṟip paṟṟi terintu koḷpavarkaḷiṉ vāḻvilum nalla tiruppumuṉi ēṟpaṭṭu vāḻvu cemmiyākum eṉpatu uṟuti.
poṟṟoṭi centil
(va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ makaḷ)
mumpi	
21-7-2017.






nakatarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ
mutaṟ patippu 1963
innūl 1969il  pāriniliyam veḷiyiṭṭa patippi mūlamākak koṇṭu veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.


patippuri
nakarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ eṉṉum innūlil eṭṭuvaki ēṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ kālam ki.pi.1600 mutal 1800 vari eṉṟu matippiṭalām. nāṭṭuvaralāṟu eṉpatu pōrc ceyalkaḷiyum aracarkaḷiṉ ceytikaḷiyum maṭṭum kuṟippataṉṟu; kuṭimakkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷiyum camutāya naṭappuk kaḷiyum kūṟavēṇṭum. avvakkālattu maṉappāṉmikaḷik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. ivvēṭukaḷ paḻaṉittalattu munnūṟṟimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka nikaḻntuvarum camaya naṭappukkaḷiyum naṭattiyavarkaḷiyum avarkaḷiṉ aṟputac ceyalkaḷiyum acceyalkaḷi viyanta aracarkaḷiyum avvaracarkaḷiṉ camaya matippiṉiyum virivākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvaṟaṅkaḷic ceytaruḷiyavarkaḷ nakarattārkaḷ; ātaliṉ nakarattār varalāṟṟiṉiyum perumiyiṉiyum ivvēṭukaḷ cuṭaṭikkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa pakkam koṇṭa ivvēṭukaḷ pāṇṭiyar, cōḻar, maturi nāyakkar, civakaṅkiccīmi marutu, putukkōṭṭit toṇṭimāṉ mutaliyōrip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ varalāṟṟāciriyar kaḷukkut tuṇi ceyyum eṉa nampukiṉṟēṉ. munnūṟṟimpatu āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ pēccut tamiḻppaṭi eḻutappaṭṭa ivvēṭukaḷ tamiḻmoḻi yārāycciyāḷarkkum nalla tuṇi ceyyum. camayam paṟṟiya pala marapukaḷ iruttaliṉ, civa camaya varalāṟṟiṟkum īṇṭuccāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. ivvaccu nūlil aṭaṅkiya ēṭukaḷiṉ vaki:-
paṭṭayattiṉ amippu muṟikaḷ
cāli, kali, tamiḻ eṉṟa mūṉṟuvaki āṇṭukaḷum tiṅkaḷ nāḷ kiḻami ōrikaḷum pala paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉār muṉpāka iṉṉār koṭutta tarmacācaṉappaṭṭayam eṉṟu eḻutumuṟiyik kāṇkiṉṟōm. viḷakkamākavum muṭivil tokuttum cācaṉa nōkkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. camayattuṟiyil yār yārkku eṉṉeṉṉa mariyātikaḷ, mariyāti varicikaḷ avvaricikkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ, aṟputa nikaḻccikaḷ, pūci naṭappukkaḷ, annaṭappukku vēṇṭum poruḷ taraṅkaḷ ellām aiyattiṟkum vaḻuviṟkum iṭamiṉṟik kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟikkuṟikkum nōkkamē ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya nōkkamākum. oru paḻaṅ kiyeḻuttup puttakappaṭiyiliruntu innūl accākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum mūlappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ ēṭukaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum.
ovvoru paṭṭayattiṟkum ettaṉi ēṭukaḷ eṉpataṉi mēl aṭṭavaṇiyiṟ kāṇalām. ivvaṟap paṇiyi ulakam uḷḷavari naṭattuvōm eṉavum, etu nili tirintālum ivvaṟanili tiriyōm eṉavum, ivvaṟattiṟku iṭiyūṟu ceytavarkaḷ iṉṉapaḻikku āḷāvārkaḷ eṉavum, utavi ceytavarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vāḻvārkaḷ eṉavum iṟutiyil viṭātu kuṟippiṭuvatu ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻakkāṟākum. iccācaṉam eḻutiṉatu iṉṉār coṟpaṭikku iṉṉāṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭi muṭikkappaṭum.  yār eḻutiṉāl cācaṉa nōkkam valuppaṭumō avarikkoṇṭu eḻutaccollum aṟivuṭimiyip pārkkiṉṟōm. paṭṭayattiṉ toṭakkattilum kaṭiciyilum murukaṉ pāṭalkaḷ iruttaluṇṭu. iṉi ovvoru paṭṭayattiṉ karuttiṉiyum curukkamākak kāṇpōm.
1. paḻaṉikkōyil naṭappu aṭṭavaṇi (pakkam 1- 27)
nēmaṅkōyil iḷanala vakuppu kuppāpiccañ ceṭṭi makaṉ kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalañ ceṉṟu uppu vaṇikañ ceytatu, paḻaṉikkōyil teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉiyil taṅkip paṇṭārattiṉ utaviyiyum avarmaṉivi, pārvatiyammāḷ utaviyiyum peṟṟatu. vēlāyuta cuvāmikku ilāpattil makamip paṇam cērttatu, piṟakōyil nakarac ceṭṭiyārkaḷum kumarappaṉōṭu uppu vāṇikattiṟkuc cērntu koṇṭatu. uppuc ceṭṭikaḷ eṉṟa peyar peṟṟatu. (kalāmaṭam)īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṭṭaḷippaṭi kumarappaṉ paḻaṉikkuk kāvaṭi kaṭṭiyatu, iṉṉiṉṉa iṭattil taṅkic ceṉṟu,aintu nāḷ makēcuvara pūci ceyvatu, ēḻu kōvil nakarattārum kāvaṭi eṭuttatu. īcāṉiya kurutēcikar kumarappaṉ mutalāṉavar paciyip pōkkiyatu, pūcikkup piramputtaṭi aruḷiyatu, kumarappaṉ campaṭṭiyil nattaliṅka nāyakkar maṉiviyiṉ vayiṟṟuvaliyip paḻaṉiyappar aruḷāṟ pōkkiyatu. nāyakkar maṉivi kumarappaṉ vīṭu tēṭivantu naṉṟi celuttiyatu, nāyakkar iraṇṭu vēlāyutam paḻaṉiyapparukkuk koṭuttatu, vēlāyuta cuvāmi pārvatiyammāḷukkum kumarappaṉ peṇcāti ciṭṭāḷukkum īcāṉiyacivācāri yārukkum āyikkuṭi camīṉtārukkum kaṉavil tōṉṟic colliyatu, camīṉtāriṉ curanōyip pōkkiyatu, maṇṭakappaṭi naṭattuvatu, paṇṭārattu maṉiyi maṭamāka vittuk koḷvatu, paṇṭārattukkuk kuṭumpattārkkum vaḻi vaḻi naṉṟi maṟavātiruppatu eṉṟa karuttukkaḷum iṉṉārukku iṉṉa koṭuppatu eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷi varaṉmuṟikaḷum ivvaṭṭavaṇiyil collappaṭṭuḷa.
maṇṭakappaṭiyil paṇṭārattukku aritip parivaṭṭamum, kumarappaṉukku alaṅkārap parivaṭṭamum, nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tirunīṟum kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉavum, nakarappotuviliruntu (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikarukkum (pātarakkuṭi) tiruppuṉal tēcikarukkum, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum, kumarappaṉukkum koṭuppatu eṉavum, iṉam oṉṟukkut tirunīṟṟuppi oṉṟu koṭuppatu eṉavum mariyātikaḷ variyaṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (pak.24)
2. ēḻunakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 28 -42)
itu teyvacikāmaṇi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum eḻutikkoṭutta tarumacācaṉam. cāli āṇṭu 1780 eṉpatu piḻiyeṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. kaliyāṇṭōṭum piṟacācaṉaṅkaḷōṭum ottiṭṭup pārkkumpōtu, cāli 1520 eṉa iruttal vēṇṭum pōlum. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, nakarattār perumikaḷ, ēḻu nakarattāriṉ irupattiraṇṭu vakuppukaḷ, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāviṟkum koṭukkavēṇṭum arici mutalāṉa poruḷaḷavukaḷ, tīrttamum tirunīṟum vaḻaṅkum mariyāti varicikaḷ, pattāntirunāḷ maṇṭakappaṭiyil koṭukkum poruḷaḷavukaḷ, taṅki varavēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ ellām iccācaṉattu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cōḻavaracaṉ nakarattār peṇṇimaruvip poṟukka muṭiyāta tontaravu ceytapaṭiyāl, iḷiyāttaṅkuṭi vanta ceytiyiyum, araca taṇṭaṉikku okkūruṭiyāṉ maṟutali koṭuttapaṭiyāl nakarakkōyilkaḷil okkūruṭiyāṉukku mutal mariyāti naṭakkiṉṟa ceytiyiyum aṟikiṉṟōm; eṉiṉum, nēmam kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalattukku uppu viṟkavantu paṭṭapirayāciyi matittu, paḻaṉittalattil mutal vipūtiyum kāḷāñciyum parivaṭṭamum kumarappaṉukku naṭakkaṭṭum eṉṟu (pak.38) eḻu nakarattār aṉivōrum orēmaṉatāyppēci muṭittaṉar eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa karuttākum. iccācaṉam nakarattār coṟpaṭikku okkūruṭiyāṉ uṭiyappa ceṭṭi aṭikkappaṉ eḻutiyatu. okkūruṭiyāṉ taṉakku uriya mutal mariyātiyip paḻaṉittalattil nēmam kumarappaṉukku viṭṭukkoṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, iccācaṉatti okkūruṭiyāṉik koṇṭu eḻutac colliṉar nakarattār eṉa aṟiyavēṇṭum. ēḻu nakarattārum taṉittaṉi āṟu kāl kūṭattil kumarappaṉik kumpiṭṭu viḻuntu tirunīṟu vāṅkikkoḷvatu eṉṟa mariyātic ceytiyum, kumarappaṉ cantatiyil mutalvaruṭam paṭṭam kaṭṭukiṉṟa vivaramum aṟiyalām.
3. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 42 - 65.)
itu cāli 1688 - kali 4868 (ki.pi.1766) ām āṇṭil kuḻanti paṇṭārattu makaṉ kumāracāmi paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum aḷitta cācaṉappaṭṭayam. mēliccācaṉam pōla yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum nakarattār perumikaḷum pirivukaḷum paṇṭārattukkum kuṭumpa vaḻiyiṉarkkum koṭukkum kaṭṭaḷikaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉ vikāvūr nāṭṭu vicayakiri vēlāyutat turiyarkaḷukku āṇ kuḻanti piṟakkum eṉṟu colliyatu, turiyiṉ paṭṭattammāḷ kuṭṭayaṉiṭam makappēṟu kēṭṭatu, kuṉṉakkuṭiyil periyamarutu pāṇṭiyar mūṉṟu kēḷvi kēṭṭut takka viṭipeṟṟatu, kuṭṭayaṉukkuk kaṇiyāḻiyum yāṉikkompum koṭuttatu eṉṟa aṟputaṅkaḷi aṟiyalām.
vicayakirit turiyum marutupāṇṭiyarum uttara viṭṭapaṭi, iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉukku aruḷāṭum kāraṇattāl, iraṇṭāvatu kāḷāñciyum mariyātik kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉṟa muṭivu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum. mariyāti varicikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum muṟikaḷum 59 - 62ām pakkam vari virivāka iṭam peṟṟuḷa. ippaṭṭayattil vicayanakarattu aracarkaḷiyum avarkaḷ vaḻiyāṇṭa nāyakkar kaḷiyum makāmaṇṭalēcuvaraṉ eḻupattēḻu pāḷiyam mutaliyavaṟṟiyum paṟṟic ciṟanta kuṟippukkaḷ uḷa (pak. 47 - 49)
4. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 66 -78)
itu cāli 1710 kali 4889 (ki.pi. 1788) ām āṇṭil kumāracāmi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum koṭutta taruma cācaṉappaṭṭayam. nakarattārkaḷiṉ perumikaḷ pirivukaḷ aintu nāḷ pūcikkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāvukkum koṭukkum arici poṉ paḻam mutaliyavi, paḷḷayattukkuk koṭukkum poruḷ aḷavukaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. veṟṟili pākku paḻam kāykaṟi kaḷōṭu pōyiliyum curuṭṭum koṭuppatuṇṭu eṉṟa oru putucceytiyi ippaṭṭayattil kaṟkiṉṟōm. nēmaṅkōvil kumarappaṉukkum iraṇikkōyil kuṭṭayaṉukkum piṟarukkum uriya mariyāti varicikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum 73 -76 ām pakkaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷa. teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉiyil muṉpirunta āṟukāl kūrik kūṭattik kaltiruppaṇi ceytu āṟukāl cavukkiyākkiyatum, 78 kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapam ceytatum kuṟikkattakka muṉṉēṟṟaṅ kaḷām. iṅṅaṉam nakarattār celavu ceytu paṇi perukki ṉālum aṉupavikkum urimi paṇṭārattu vaḻiyiṉarkku eṉṟum uṇṭu. vayiravaṉ kōyil vēlappa ceṭṭi vayiravaṉ tiruppaṇi vēliyil mikkamuyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭataṉālum, cuvāmi vayiravaṉiṭam nēril tōṉṟip pēciyataṉālum (pak.76) nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟāvatu mariyāti vayiravaṉukku uriyatu eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum.
5. piṟāmali ātīṉam naṭappu aṭṭavaṇi (pakkam 79 -85)
paḻaṉikkōyil iṭumpaṉmali mutal ūr varum variyil īcāṉiya civācāriyār, paṇṭāram, kumarappaṉ nakarattār ceyyavēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉa kaṭami eṉṟu caṉṉitāṉam colliyapōtu ātīṉa mariyāti taṉakkum vēṇṭum eṉṟu iraṇikkōyil tiruvēṭpū ruṭiyāṉ kuṭṭayaṉ kēṭṭāṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷik kalantālōcittu, tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭiyil naṭakkiṟa paṭṭuparivaṭṭamum antac celavum kuṭṭayaṉ taṉatāka irukkaṭṭum eṉṟu cuvāmi colliyatu. caṉṉitāṉam ūrukku varumpōtu kuṭṭayaṉukku mūṉṟāvatu paṭṭup pītāmparam koṭukkiṉṟatu.
6. ātīṉam paṭṭāpiṭēka aṭṭavaṇi (pakkam 86 -90)
kumarappaṉ paḻaṉiyāṇṭavar aṉukkirakam peṟṟa paṭiyāṉum, ātīṉattukku vēṇṭum kāriyaṅkaḷum uḻippum ceytapaṭiyālum ātīṉappaṭṭāpiṭēkattu. iraṇṭāvatākak kumarappaṉ paṭṭu cāttukiṉṟatu eṉṟu īcāṉakurutēcikar moḻintār. itaṉi irupattoru kōttirattu nakarattār kaḷum cariyeṉa ottukkoṇṭaṉar. vaḻakkampōl naṭakkavēṇumēyoḻiyap putuvaḻakkam ceyyappaṭātu eṉṟu iḷiyāttāṅkuṭi paṭṭaṇacuvāmi vakuppu aruṇācalañ ceṭṭi aṇṇāmali maṭṭum caṉṉitāṉattiṉ tiruvaṭiyil viḻuntu maṟuppuk kūṟiṉār. kumarappaṉ perumiyik kurutēcikar eṭutturittapiṉ aṇṇāmaliyum ottuk koṇṭār. ottukkoṇṭataṟku uṟutiyāka aṇṇā maliyiyē aṭṭavaṇi eḻutumpaṭi īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṇittār.

7. maṭavālayat tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 91 - 100)
itu cāli 1727 kali 4906 (ki.pi.1805) āṇṭil nākappa paṇṭārattu makaṉ kuḻantivēlup paṇṭārattukku ēḻunakarattār aṉivōrum koṭutta cācaṉappaṭṭaya mākum. muṉṉiccācaṉaṅkaḷ pōlap paṇṭārattukkuk koṭukkum naṭappukkaḷum piṟavum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. iṭam pōtāmiyiṉāl aṭuttamaṉi mōḷiyattēvaṉ vakiyaṟā iṭatti vilikku vāṅki ākkupuriyum muppattoṉpatu kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapamum kaṭṭiya vaḷarcci kuṟippiṭattakum. maṭattiṟkāka vāṅkiya immaṉikaḷum paṇṭārattiṉ anupōkattiṟku uriyaṉavē piḷḷiyārkōyil vakuppu muttappaṉukkuc civappaṭṭiyil paḻaṉiyāṇṭavaruṭiya aruḷvanta ceytiyum, toṇṭimāṉ aracar muttappaṉ vīṭṭiṟku vantu niṉitta kāriyattik kēṭṭu makiḻntu cālvi mutaliyaṉa koṭutta ceytiyum, muttappaṉ kēṭṭuk koṇṭapaṭi kōvil vīṭṭukkut tāmpūravēl koṭutta ceytiyum, nēmaṅkōvil paḻaṉiyappaṉ paḻaṉikkōvilukkuk kāvaṭi eṭuttu varumpōtu inta vēlāyutattiyum vittuppūci ceytuvarumpaṭi colliya ceytiyum, itaṉic colvataṟkākap paḻaṉiyappaṉi araṇmaṉikku aḻittuvantu mariyāti ceyta ceytiyum ippaṭṭayattukku mukkiya ceytikaḷākum. (pak. 96 -98) tēvakōṭṭi rattiṉavēlāyutamum kuṉṟakkuṭi vēlāyutamum cērttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
8. nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar paṭṭayam (pakkam 100 -116)
itu cāli 1549 kali 4730 (ki.pi.1627) ām āṇṭil ēḻu nakarattār muṉpākak kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam nirampa vaḻaṅkiya tēcikar (tuḷāvūr maṭam) koṭutta paṭṭayam. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷum nāyakkar peyarkaḷum muṉṉark kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēcikar perumi, nakarattār perumi, kōttiram, kulacēkarapura cavuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ nakarattārkaḷi naṟkuṭiyākat taṉ cīmikku aḻittatu, nakarattāriṉ veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ cōḻaṉ uttira viṭṭum (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikari toḻa maṟuttatu eṅkaḷ nirampavaḻakiya kurutēcikar (tuḷāmaṭam) pāṇṭināṭṭiṟku vantāl nāṅkaḷum pōvōm eṉṟu urittatu, veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuruviṭam muṟiyiṭṭatu, cōḻaṉ kurutēcikari varavaḻittup pāṇṭiyaṉ pala kirāmaṅkaḷ taruvāṉ eṉṟu moḻintu irupattiraṇṭu kōttirattu nakarattār āṇkaḷum veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷum pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟku vantatu, pāṇṭiyaṉ ellōriyum etir koṇṭaḻittu mākāṇaṅkaḷ carvamāṉiyamāka aḷittatu. kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam amintatu mutalāṉa nakarattār varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷi ippaṭṭayattut teḷivāka aṟiyalām.
nēmaṅkōyil kumarappaṉum avaṉ maṉivi veḷḷāṭṭi ciṭṭāḷum uppumāṟi eṭutta makamiyāl inta ātīṉamum piṟa tarumaṅkaḷum naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉavum, paḻaṉittalattilpōla intattalattilum nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tīrttam tirunīṟu kumarappaṉukkuk koṭuppatu eṉavum nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar uttaravuppaṭi nakarattār aṉivōrum uṭaṉpaṭṭa ceytiyi ippaṭṭayam mukkiyamākac collukiṉṟatu. mariyāti varicikaḷ 114 -15 ām pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum naṭimuṟikaḷum
paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟittuḷ naṭappukkaḷ iṉṟum perumpālum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. kālappōkkil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkakkūṭum. ellāp paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum mutal mariyātikkuriyavarāṉa nēmam kumarappaṉ vaḻi vantōrē iṉṟu neṟkuppi ku.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉarāvar. iraṇṭām mariyātikku uriya aruḷāṭi iraṇikkōyil pe.kuṭṭayaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kaṇṭaṉūr kupa.cu. vīṭṭiṉar nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟām mariyātikku uriya vayiravaṉkōyil vayiravaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kārikkuṭi kuma.peri.cu.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉar. tāmpūravēl peṟṟa piḷḷiyōr kōvil muttappaṉ vaḻivantavarē iṉṟu mēliccivapuri āṟu.muru. kuṭumpattiṉar iṅṅaṉam teyva aruḷpeṟṟa nakarattār nālvar vaḻivantōr ālpōl taḻittu vāḻntu varutaliṉ, muṉṉōr paṇikaḷ iṭiyaṟātu kākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uṇartal vēṇṭum. ikkuṭumpattārkaḷ tam kuḻantikaḷukku murukaṉ peyarkaḷiyē vippar eṉpatum niṉiyattakum.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum tamiḻum
ēṟakkuṟiya munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉtōṉṟiya iccācaṉat tamiḻ palavakiyālum ūṉṟi ārāyvataṟku uriyatu eṉṟu maṭṭum īṇṭuc cuṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻ urinaṭi varalāṟṟil iccācaṉa naṭi iṭampeṟat takuvatu. oru perum puttaka aḷaviṟku oppāṉa iccācaṉap paṭṭayaṅkaḷ pēcuvatu pōṉṟa tamiḻ naṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷa. oṇṇu, reṇṭu, mūṇu, paṉireṇṭu, aruci, kuṭuttu, rācā, pōyi, tuṇṇuttuppi, matiyāri, ciṟicākkiṉi, vīṭṭukkup pōratu, pokkucam, naccēttiram eṉappala marūuccoṟkaḷi nirampakkāṇalām. akkālattut tamiḻccoṟkaḷi eṅṅaṉam olittārkaḷ eṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷalām. māṟi (viṟṟu) atumērikku (atupōla) paraṅkaḷ (paratēcikaḷ) vaḷami (vaḻakkam) peṇṇāṭṭi (peṇṭāṭṭi) neṭṭeḻuttu (kiyeḻuttu) eṉṟa coṟkaḷiyum, aṭakeṭuttal, aritipparivaṭṭam, alaṅkārap parivaṭṭam, vēṟpaṭi, aruḷāṭi mutalāṉa coṟkaḷiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. uṇṭumā, meḻukivittuvi. nāṉum varuka, varuvōmākavum eṉṟa toṭar muṭipukaḷiyum aṟikiṉṟōm.
nakarattārkaḷ oḷaviyiṉ tamiḻppāṭṭukkup poṟpāṭakam koṭuttārkaḷ eṉavum, varappuyara eṉṟu oḷavi uḻavarkaḷi vāḻttiyatu pōla maṭiccīli peruka eṉṟu vaṇikañceyyum innakarattārkaḷi vāḻtti varaṅkoṭuttār eṉavum ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. nāyakka maṉṉaṉ muttamiḻ aṇaṅkaṉ eṉavum, vaḷḷuvamaṟiyuṅ kaṟṟu muppāl moḻiyumpaṭiyē aṟivi aṟintu allavi kaṭintu nallavi nāṭṭik kuṭitaḻikka āṇṭāṉ eṉavum moḻikiṉṟaṉa. nāṅkaḷ ēḻu nakarattārkaḷ naṭṭakalliyum iṭṭacūlamum aḻikka māṭṭōm; makāmērukiri cāyntapōtilum, kaṭal ēḻum vaṟṟiṉa pōtilum, kāvēri vaṟaṇṭa pōtilum, cantirāḷ cūriyāḷ teṟku vaṭakkāṉapōtilum nāṅkaḷ nakaram poycolla māṭṭōm eṉavarum uṟutippāṭukaḷ nakarattāriṉ nāṇayattip paṟicāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ūḻi peyariṉum tām peyarār cāṉṟāṇmikku
āḻi yeṉappaṭu vār
eṉṟa vaḷḷuvamaṟippaṭi vāḻntavarkaḷ nakarattārkaḷ eṉpataṉi inta aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ kāṭṭikkoṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭiyōr tam poyyāp paṇpiṉiyum kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉiyum pukaḻiṉiyum oḻuṅkiṉiyum aṟitaṟku ippaṭṭayanūl ārāycciyāḷar kippaṭṭupparavum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ. nakarattār camutāyam tamiḻpperuñ camutāyattiṉ oru pakutiyātaliṉ, ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tamiḻiṉattiṉ nākarika vaḷarcciyik kāṇa muyalvārkkut tuṇiceyyum eṉṟu cuṭṭikkāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
peṟutaṟkariya tammuṉṉōriṉ tarumap paṭṭayaṅkaḷi yāvarum aṟiyum vaṇṇam accu nūlvaṭivākki utaviya tiru.ku.paḻa.cu.paḻa.cuppiramaṇiya ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷukkut tamiḻulakam mikka kaṭappāṭu uṭiyatu. ellā nakarattār kaḷuṭiya poruḷutaviyiṉālum ivveḷiyīṭu varukiṉṟatu. nakarattārkaḷukku ellāt tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ naṉṟiyuriyatu.
ṭākṭar. va.cupa.māṇikkam
attāṭci uri
paḻanimali vēlāyuta cuvāmiyārukku naṭantu varukiṟa tarma aṭṭavaṇi paṟṟiya “nakarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ” ēḻu aṭaṅkiya nūl oṉṟu neṟkuppi tiru. paḻa.cu. paḻa. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷāl 22-7-61il muṉṉuriyil aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttup pirati oṉṟum ataṟkuc cariyāka acciṭap peṟṟa accup pirati oṉṟum eṉṉiṭam kāṭṭap peṟṟatāka appōtu eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ.
mēṟkaṇṭa kiyeḻuttu -accup piratikaḷukku mūlamāṉa eṭṭup piratikaḷi ippoḻutu paḻaṉi paṇṭārattiyā vīṭṭiliruntu tēṭi eṭuttu vāṅkik koṇṭu vantu cupa. avarkaḷ kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. cuvaṭikaḷ paḻami yāṉaviyākavum, ki.pi. 1608il eḻutappeṟṟa cuvaṭiyil okkūruṭiyāṉ uṭiyappa ceṭṭi aṭikkappaṉ eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum 1766il eḻutap peṟṟa cuvaṭiyil iḷanalamuṭiyāṉ aṭikkappa ceṭṭi aruṇācalam eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum 1788il eḻutappeṟṟa cuvaṭiyil piḷḷiyār paṭṭik kōyil aruṇācalañ ceṭṭi kumarappaṉ eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum 1805il eḻutap peṟṟa cuvaṭiyil iluppikkuṭi leṭcumaṇañ ceṭṭi aruṇācalam eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum uṭiyaṉavākavum kiyeḻuttup piratiyil eḻutiyavarkaḷ kavaṉakkuṟivāl viṭṭuviṭṭa cila coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kāṭṭuvaṉavākavu mirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṭṭup piratiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kāṭṭuvaṉavākavumirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṭṭup piratiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷ accup piratiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷōṭu poruḷ māṟupāṭuṭiyaṉavākap perumpālumilli eṉṟālum ēṭṭup pirati kiṭittataṉāl muṉṉar teḷivu peṟātirunta ciṟcila karuttukkaḷ ippōtu teḷivu peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. eṭuttukāṭṭukku accup piratiyiṉ 3-ām pakkattu 16,17 varikaḷil “mutal oru paṅkum iraṇṭu paṅkum uṇṭāccutu” eṉṟiruppatu ēṭṭup piratiyil “mutal orupaṅkum lāpam iraṇṭu paṅkum uṇṭāccutu” eṉṟu kāṇappeṟuvati niṉivūṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
immūla ātaravik koṇṭu ivikaḷiṟ kaṇṭa naṭimuṟikaḷ toṭakka kālantoṭṭu iṭiyil evvita māṟṟamumiṉṟi nikaḻntu vantavi eṉpati uṟutiyāka aṟintu koḷḷalām. putitākak kiṭitta paḻiya ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷil uḷḷapaṭi paṭṭayaṅkaḷi, aṭutta patippu veḷiyiṭum pōtu acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttuvārkaḷ.
maṟintirunta mūlap piratiyi veḷippaṭuttit tanta srī ñāṉateṇṭāyutap perumāṉ nāṭṭuk kōṭṭi nakarattārkaḷukkum avarkaḷiṉ piratinitikaḷākap paḻani aṉṉatāṉa maṭattiṉ aṟappaṇikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu toṇṭupurintu varum neṟkuppi ku.paḻa. kuṭumpattārukkum, kaṇṭaṉūr ku.pa.cu. kuṭumpattārukkum kārikkuṭi kuma.peri.cu. paḻa. kuṭumpattārukkum paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭa naṭi muṟikaḷiṉpaṭi peṟum aruṭ piracātaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilāka ellā nalaṉkaḷiyum vaḻaṅkiyaruḷuvārāka!
nalam perukuka.
tēvakōṭṭi	vē. irāmanātaṉ,
5-3-363	pālakavi.
